- **collab**: Async collaborative brainstorming between multiple human+Claude pairs. - **collab**: Multi-AI collaboration for tasks requiring multiple perspectives, discussion, or negotiation. Use when: user explicitly requests collaboration (negotiate, discuss, multiple angles, review from different perspectives), task benefits from diverse viewpoints (review, critique, evaluation), or task needs specialist discussion. Examples: 'review this from multiple angles', 'negotiate and handle this issue', 'discuss pros and cons', 'get different perspectives on this'. ([Done-0/unitor](https://github.com/Done-0/unitor/blob/HEAD/plugins/unitor/commands/collab.md) · ★1)

- **command-args-validation.doctest**: Every command validates its untyped `args` bag through `parseCommandArgs` (`command-runner.ts`) — the dispatch boundary. A wrong-typed field is a `CommandArgsError`, surfaced by `runCommand` as a clean `{ success: false }` result naming the field — never silently dropped, never a stack trace. Presence checks stay with the command (friendlier error text), so a missing field gets the command's own m ([ianb/beebox](https://github.com/ianb/beebox/blob/HEAD/beebox/test/core/commands/command-args-validation.doctest.md) · ★1)

- **commit-suggest**: Suggest a Conventional Commits message from the current staged + unstaged changes. Read-only — runs only allowlisted git commands, never commits, never pushes, never adds AI co-author trailers. Output is plain text the user pastes. Triggers: "suggest a commit", "commit message", "commit msg", "write a commit", "gen conventional commit", "/commit". Classifies type (feat/fix/chore/refactor/docs/test/perf); subject under 50 chars; body only when "why" isn't obvious.
